का . पा . 1 344. – केन्द्रीय सरकार का विहित प्राधि 
कारी द्वारा उसे प्रस्तुत की गई रिपोर्ट । ( नीचे प्राकृति देखिए ) 
पर विचार करने के पश्चात , समाधान हो गया है कि उक्त 
रिपोर्ट में वर्णित माडल बाट और माप मानक अधिनियम 
1976 ( 1976 का 60 ) और बाट और माप मानक 
( माडल अनुमोदन ) नियम , 1987 के उपबंधों के अनुरूप 
है और इस बात की संभावना है कि वह लगातार प्रयोग 
की अवधि में बनाए रखेगा और विभिन्न परिस्थितियों में 
उपयुक्त सेंधा देता रहेगा ; 

प्रतः, केन्द्रीय सरकार , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 36 की 
उपधारा ( 7 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए 
मध्यम यथार्थता वर्ग III की सी के टी 560 सिरीज टाइप 
के " वे इस " ब्रांड नाम वाले स्वतः सूचक गैर-स्वचालित 
यांत्रिक तुला चौकी को अंकीय संप्रदर्श वाले उपकरणों से 
रूपातरण करने के लिए रूपांतरण किट के माउल का ( जिसे 
इसमें इसके पश्चात माडल कहा गया है ) जिसका विनिर्माण 
मैसर्स वे उस इलेक्ट्रोनिक एण्ड इंडस्ट्रियल सविसेज प्राइवेट

का . श्रा , 1348 : - - केन्द्रीय सरकार ने पट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का 
अर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन जारी की गयी भारत सरकार 
के पैट्रोलियम और प्राकृतिक गैस मंबालय की अधिमूचना संख्या का . आ . 53, तारीख 11 जनवरी , 1997 तारा 
पश्चिमी बंगाल राज्य में हल्दिया से बिहार राज्य में बरौनी तक पैट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए पाइपलाइन बिछाने 
के प्रयोजन हेतु उस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अन सूची में विनिदिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन करने के 
अपने प्राशय की घोषणा की थी । 

और, उक्त अधिसूचना की प्रतियां जनता को तारीख 1 फरवरी, 1997 को उपलब्ध करा दी गयी थी ; 

और , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अनुसरण में सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने केन्द्रीय सरकार को अपनी 
रिपोर्ट प्रस्तुत कर दी है ; 

और, केन्द्रीय सरकार का उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात यह समाधान हो गया है कि इस अधि 
सचना से संलग्न अन सूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का मजन पाइपलाइन बिछाने के लिए किया 
जाना चाहिए । 


[ भाग JI -- खंड 3 ( ii )] भारत का राजपत्र : मई 24, 1997 |ज्येष्ठ 3,1919 

2613 
- - - - - - --- - -- - -- - 

अतः, अब , केन्द्रीय सरकार , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शशितयों का प्रयोग करते 
हुए , घोषणा करती है कि इस अधिसूचना से मंलग्न अनुसूपी में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग का अभिकार अजित 
किया जाता है ; 

यह भी कि केन्द्रीय मरकार उक्त धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त, शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, यह निवेश 
देती है कि उक्त भूमि के उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने के बजाय सभी बिलगमों में मका इंडियन 
शॉयल कारपोरेशन लिमिटेड में निहित होगा ।

S . O . 1348 . — Whereas by the notification of the Government of India in the Ministry of Petroleum and Natural 
Gas No . S . O . 53, dated the 11th January , 1997, issued under sub -section (1) of section 3 of the Petroleum and 
Minerals Pipelines (Acquisition of Right of User in Land ) Act, 1962 (50 of 1962) the Central Government . 
its intention to acquire the right of user in the lands specificd in the schedule appended to that notification for the 
purpose of laying pipelines for the transport of petroleum from Haldia in the State of West Bengal to Baranoia 
the State of Bihar ; 

And whereas , the copies of the said notification were made available to the public on the 1st day of Febru , 
1997 ; 

And whereas, the Competent Authority in pursuance of sub -section ( 1) of the section 6 of the said Art 
submitted his report to the Central Government ; 

And whereas , the Central Government after considering the said report is satisfied that the right of war 
the land specificd in the Schedule appended to this notification should be acquired for laying pipelines . 

Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by sub -section (1 ) of section 6 of the said Act, the Cen 
Government hereby declares that the right of user in the lands specified in the Schedule appended to this noti 
fication are licreby acquired ;

का . पा . 1349 .-- - केन्द्रीय सरकार ने पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन ( मि में उपयोग के अधिकार का 
अर्बन ) अधिनियम 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन जारी की गरी भारत 
मरकार के पंद्रोलियम और प्राकृतिक गौम मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना गण्या का . ग्रा . 2989, तारीद 25 
1996 वारा , पश्चिमी बंगाल राज्य में हल्दिया मे बिहार राज्य में बरीती तक पेट्रोलियन के परिवहन के नि : 
पाइप लाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन हेतु उस अधिसूचना में संलग्न प्रन मूवी में विनिदिष्ट ममि में उपयोग के अधिकार का 
अर्जन करने के अपने प्राशय की घोषणा की थी । 


और , उक्त अधिसूचना की प्रतियां जनता को नारीव 16 दिसम्बर, 1996 हो उन कामो गोपी । 


और, उबल अधिनियम की धारा 6की उधारा ( 1 ) के ग्रसरण में मम Hff3rd मोर हो अपनी 
रिपोर्ट प्रस्तुत कर दी है । 

और, केन्द्रीय सरकार का उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चा । यह ममावान हो गह न पवना 
में लग्न अनसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रजन पाइपलाइन बिछाने के लि ! किया जाना चाहिए । 

ग्रतः अब, केन्द्रीय सरकार , उक्त अधिनियम की धाग 6की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
प , घोषणा करती है कि जम अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनसूची में विनिदिर समितों में योग का अधिकार गावात 
किया जाता है । 

और, यह भी कि केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदान शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हो , 
यह निवेश देती है कि उक्त भमि के उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने के बजाय मभी विकलगमों मे 
41 मत इंडियन ऑयल कारपोरेशन लिमिटेड में निहित होगा ।

New Delhi, the 14th May, 1997 


S . O . 1349 . - Whereas by the notification of the Government of India in the Ministry of Petroleum and Natural 
Gas No. S. O . 2989, dated the 26th October , 1996 , issued under sub -section (1 ) of section 3 of the Petroleum and 
Minerals Pipelines (Acquisition of Right of User in Land) Act, 1962 (50 of 1962) the Central Government declared 
its intention to acquire the right of user in the lands specified in the Schedule appended to that notification for the 
purpose of laying pipelines for the transport of petroleum from Haldia in the State of West Bengal to Barauni in 
the State of Bihar ; 


And whereas, the copies of the said notification were made available to the public on the 16th day of December 
1996 ; 


And whereas, the competent Authority in pursuance of sub - section ( 1) of the section 6 of the said Act has 
submitted his report to the Central Government; 


And whereas, the Central Government after considering the said report is satisfied that the right of user in the 
land specified in the Schedule appended to this notification should be acquired for laying pipelines ; 


Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by sub -section ( 1 ) of section 6 of the said Act, the Central 
Government hereby declares that the right of user in the lands specified in the Schedule appended to this notifica 
tion are hereby acquired ; 


And further, in exercise of the powers conferred by the sub -section (4 ) of the said section , the Centraj 
Government hereby directs that the right of user in the said lands shall instead of vesting in the Central Government 
vest, in the Indian Oil Corporation Limited free from all encumbrances ,

K . C. KATOCH, Under Secy . 

नई दिल्ली , 14 मई , 1997 
का . प्रा . - 1350. - - केन्द्रीय सरकार , ने , पैट्रोलियम और खनिज पाईपलाईन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का पर्जन ) 
अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) (जिसे इसमें इसके पश्चात् उक्त अधिनियम कहा गया है ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा 
( 1 ) के अधीन जारी की गई भारत सरकार के पैट्रोलियम और प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना संख्या का . या . 2982 
तारीख 26 अक्तूबर , 1996, द्वारा पैट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिये पाईपलाईन बिछाने के प्रयोजनार्थ उक्त प्राधिरा चना से 
संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार के अर्जन के अपने प्राशय की घोषणा की थी । 

सचना की प्रतियां जनता को तारीख 18 नवम्बर 1996 को उपलब्ध करा दी गई थी ; 
और उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अनुसरण में सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने केन्द्रीय सरकार को अपनी 
रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 

और केन्द्रीय सरकार का उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात् यह समाधान हो गया है कि इस अधिसचना से 
संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन किया जाये ; 

प्रतः, अब , केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए 
अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार अजित करने की घोषणा करती है । 

यह और कि केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हए यह निदेश देती है कि उक्त भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने के बजाय सभी विल्लंगमों 
से मुक्त इंडियन आयल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड में निहित होगा ।

नई दिल्ली, 14 मई, 1997 
कामा० 1351 : - - केन्द्रीय सरकार ने , पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन ) 
अधिनियम 1962 1992 का 50 ) ( जिसे इसमें इसके पश्चात् उक्त अधिनियम कहाँ गया है ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन जारी की 
गई भारत सरकार के पेट्रोलियम और प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना संख्या का०मा० 2981 तारीख 26 अक्तूबर, 1996 , 
द्वारा पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजनार्थ उक्त अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में 
उपयोग के अधिकार के अर्जन के अपने प्राशय की घोषणा की थी । 

और राजपत्रित अधिसूचना की प्रतियां जनता को तारीख 18 नवम्बर, 1996 को उपलब्ध करा दी गई थी ; 
और उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अनुसरण में सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने केन्द्रीय सरकार को अपनी रिपोर्ट 


नता 


और केन्द्रीय सरकार का उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात् यह समाधान हो गया है कि इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनु 
सूची में विनिदिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन किया जाए ; 

अतः अब , केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए अधि 
मचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिदिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार अजित करने की घोषणा करती है ; 

अत :, अब, केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए अधि 
सूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार अजित करने की घोषणा करती है । 

यल और कि केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए यह 
निदेगा देती है कि उक्त भमि में उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने के बजाय सभी विल्लंगमों से मक्त इंडियन . 
ग्रॉयल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड में निहित होगा ।

New Delhi, the 14th May , 1997 
S . O . 1351 . . . - Whereas by the notification of the Government of India in the Ministry of Petroleum and Na 
turalGas No. S . O . 2981 dated the 26th October, 1996 , issued under sub -section ( 1 ) of section 3 of tho Petroleum and 
Minerals Pipelines ( Acquisition of Right of User in Land ) Act, 1962 (50 of 1962) the Contral Government declared 
its intention to acquire the right of user in lands specified in the schedule appended to that notification for the pur 
pose of laying pipelines for the transport of crude from Haldia in the Statc of West Bengal to Barauni in the State of 
Bihar; 


And whereas, the copies of the said notification were made available to the public on the 18th November, 
1996 ; 


And whereas, the competent authority in pursuance of sub - section (1) of section 6 of the said Act bas sub 
mitted his roport to the Central Govornment ; 


And whercas the Central Government after considering the said report is satisfied that the right of user in lands 
for laying pipelines specificd in the Schedule appended to this notifictaion should be acquirod ; 


Now , therefore , in exorcise of the powers conferred by the sub -section ( 1) of section 6 of the said Act , the Cen 
tral Government hereby declaros that the right of user in the lands specified in the Schedule apponded to 
this notification are hereby acquired ; 

And further, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub - section (4 ) of section 6 of the said Act, the Contral 
Government hereby directs the right of user in the said lands for laying pipolinos shall instead of vosting in the Cen 
tral Government, vest , in the Indian Oil Corporation Limited free from all oncumbranccs.

का आ . 1352. -~ - केन्द्रीय सरकार ने पैट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन ) 
अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन जारी की गई भारत सरकार के पैदोलियम 
और प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना संख्या का . प्रा . 3238, तारीख 23 नवम्बर , 1996 द्वारा , पश्चिमी बंगाल 
राज्य में हल्दिया से बिहार राज्य में बगैनी तक पैट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिये पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन हेतु उस 
अधिमथना से संलग्न अनुमची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन करने के अपने ग्राशय की घोषणा की थी । 


प्रौर , उक्त अधिसूचना की प्रतियां जनता को तारीख 02 जनवरी , 1997 को उपलब्ध करा दी गई थी ; 


और, उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधाग ( 1 ) के अनुसरण में मक्षम प्राधिकारी ने केन्द्रीय सरकार को अपनी 
रिपोर्ट प्रस्तुत कर दी है । 


और. केन्द्रीय सरकार का उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात् यह समाधान हो गया है कि इस अधिसूचना मे . 
संलग्न अनसची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन पाइपलाइन बिछाने के लिये किया जाना चाहिये । 


गत प्रय . केन्द्रीय सरकार , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदन शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए , 
राणा करती है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित किया जाता है । 


और यह भी कि केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए , यह निर्देश 

कि उक्त भमि के उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने के बजाय सभी विल्लंगमों स मक्त इंडियन 
प्रायल कारपोरेशन लिमिटेड में निहित होगा ।

का . अ. . 1 35 3. ---- केन्द्रीय कार, में पोलियन और जनपशाईन ( भूमि में अपोग के अधिकार का अर्जन ) 
अधिनियम 1966 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धार! 3 की उनधार ! ( 1 ) के अधीन जारी की गई भारत सरकार के पैट्रोनिम और 
प्राकृतियः गैग मन्त्रालय की सधि बना संख्या का . . 2985, नसीन 26 अक्तूबर , 1996 हार। परिबनी बंगात रम में 
हरिविर - मेवगनी ला पैट्रोलियम के 

पापिन्नाईन बिछाने के प्रयोजन हेतु उम अधिसूचना 
में संभाग अन पी में विनिदिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार अर्जन करने के लिये मोमासन की योगा को यो ; 

और, उमा अधिसूचना की प्रतियां जतना को नारीख 9 दिसनर, 1336 को उपलब्ध करा दी गई थी । 

और, उक्त प्राधिनियम की धा ! 6 को उपधारा ( 1 ) के अनमरण में मम प्रारिमारी ने केन्द्रीय सरकार को अपनी 
रिपोर्ट न कर दी है ; 

ओर, केन्द्रीय सरकार का उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विकार करने के पश्चात् यह समाधान हो गया है कि इस प्रकार से 
मंलग्न अनमची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि के उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन पाईपलाईन विशने के लिये किया जाना चाहिये । 


अाः , अब , केन्द्रीय सरकार, उमा अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रबन गतिमों का पाइप 
घोषणा करती है कि इस प्राधिसूचना में मंगल गानुसूची में विनिदिष्ट भूनि में उपयोग का ग्र 

__ और यह भी कि केटीभरकार उक्त धाम को उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वार प्रदान कितनों का प्रयोग करते हए , यह निर्देश 
देती है कि कर अमि के योग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहिन होने के बजाम भभी गिनानां ना इंडियन 
पागल कारपोरेशन लिमिटेड में निहित होगः ।

ment hereby directs that the right of user in the said lands shall instead of vesting in the CentralGovernment, vest

का . पा . 1 354. ...- केन्द्रीय सरकार , ने पैट्रोलियम और खनिज पाईपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन ) 
अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन जारी की गई भारत सरकार के पैट्रोलियम और 
प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसुचना संख्या का , पा . 2469 , तारीख 24 अगस्त , 1996 द्वारा , पश्चिमी बंगाल राज्य में 
हल्दिया से बिहार राज्य में बरौनी तक पैट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिये पाईपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन हेतु उस अधिसूचना 
से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिदिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन करने के अपने प्राणय की घोषणा की थी ; 


और, उक्त अधिसूचना की प्रतियां जनता को तारीख 10 अक्तूबर , 1996 को उपलब्ध करा दी गई थी 

और, उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अनुसरण में सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने केन्द्रीय सरकार को अपनी 
रिपोर्ट प्रस्तुत कर दी है ; 

और , केन्द्रीय सरकार का उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात् यह समाधान हो गया है कि इस अधिसूचना में 
संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन पाईपलाइन बिछाने के लिये किया जाना चाहिये । 


अत:, अब , केन्द्रीय सरकार, उमस अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हुए, घोषणा करती है कि हम अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित किया जाता है । 

और , यह भी कि केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त धाग की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, यह निर्देश देती 
है कि उक्त भूमि के उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने के बजाय मभी विल्लंगमों से मुक्त इंडियन प्रायल 
कारपोरेशन लिमिटेड में निहित होगा ।

के . सी . कटोच, अवर सचिव 
New Delhi, the 14th May , 1997 
S. O . 1354. - Whereas by the notification of the Government of India in the Ministry of Petroleuin and 
Natural Gas No. S. O . 2469, dated the 24th August, 1996 , issued under sub -section (1) of section 3 of the Petroleum 
and Minerals Pipelines (Acquisition of Right of User in Land ) Act, 1962 ( 50 of 1962) the Central Government dec 
clared its intention to acquire the right of user in the lands specified in the Schedule appended to that notification 
for the purpose of laying pipelines for the transport of petroleum from Haldia in the State of West Bengal to Barauni 
in the State of Bihar ; 

And whereas, the copies of the said notification were made available to the public on the 10th day of October, 
1996 ; 

And whereas, the Competent Authority in pursuance of sub- section (1) of the section 6 of the said Act has 
submitted his report to the Central Government ; 

And whereas, the Central Government after considering the said report is satisfied that the right of user in 
the land specified in the Schedule appended to this notification should be acquired for laying pipelines ; 

Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by sub -section (1) of section 6 of the said Act , the Central 
Government hereby declares that the right of user in the lands specified in the Schedule appended to this notification 
are hereby acquired ; 

___ And further , in exercise of the powers conferred by the sub-section ( 4 ) of the said section , the Central Govern 
ment hereby directs that the right of user in the said lands shall instead of vesting in the Central Government, vest, 
in the Indian Oil Corporation Limited free from all cncumbrances .

का मा . 1 355. - - केन्द्रीय सरकार ने दीनि और खनिजपाइपलाइन ( मि में उपगीगांधकाकाअर्जन ) अधिनियम , 

उपधा ( अधीन जारी की गई भाग भरमार के नियम और प्राकृनिक जन 
मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना संसः कः . या . 588, ख मार्च , 1936 , पश्चिमी साल में हदियः बिहार राज्य 
में बरौनी तक पैट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए पापा बिछाने के प्रयोजन हतु उस अधिनचना में मंगामा प्रसूची में विनिदिष्ट 
भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रार्जन करने के अपने अाग की घोषणा की थी । 


और, उक्त अधिसूचना की प्रतियां जनता को नानी 16 दिसम्बर, 1996 को उपलब्ध ना.२ ) दी गई थी ; 

और, उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की धार ( 1 ) के अनुमरण में सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने केन्द्रीय सरकार को अपनी रिपोर 
प्रस्तुत कर दी है ; 


और, केन्द्रीय सरकार को उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात् यह समाधान हो गया है कि इस अधिसूचना गे मलग्न 
अनुमूची में बिनिदिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का आगन पाइपलाइन बिछाने के लिए किया जाना चाहिए । 

प्रतः, अब, केन्द्रीय सरकार, का अधिनियम की धार की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए , घोषणा करती है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न असमुची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित किमाता 


और , यह भी कि केन्द्रीय सरकार का धार को दुरधाम 4 बारा प्रदत्त शक्तिमा का प्रयोग करना , यह निदेश देती 
है कि उक्त भूमि के उपयोग कर अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने के बजाय सभी विलनगमों में मजन इंडियन ऑयलर 
पोरेशन लिमिटेड में निहित होगा ।

New Delhi, the 14th May, 1997 
S .O . 1355. - Whereas by the notification of the Government of India in the Ministry of Petroleum and 
Natural Gas No. S . O . 588 dated the 2nd March , 1996 , issued under sub - section (1) of section 3 of the Petroleum 
and Minerals Pipelines (Acquisition of Right of User in Land ) Act , 1962 (50 of 1962 ) the Central Government de 
clared its intention to acquire the right of user in the lands specified in the Schedule appended to that notification 
for the purpose of laying pipelines for the transport of petroleum from Haldia in the State of West Bengal to Barauni 
in the State of Bihar ; 

And whereas, the copies of the said notification were made available to the public on the 16th day of December, 
1996 ; 


And whereas, the Competent Authority in pursuance of sub -section (1) of the section 6 of the said Act has 
submitted his report to the Central Government; 


And whereas , the Central Government after considering the said report is satisfied that the right of user in 
the land specified in the Schedule appended to this notification should be acquired for laying pipelines; 

Now , therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub -section ( 1) of section 6 of the said Act,the Central 
Government hereby declares that the right of user in the lands specified in the Schedule appended to this notification 
are hereby acquired : 


And further , in exercise of the powers conferred by the sub-section (4 ) of the said section , the Central Govern 
ment hereby directs that the right of user in the said lands shall instead of vesting in the Central Government, vest, 
in the Indian Oils Corporation Limited free from all encumbrances .

2 . That , due to your aforesaid acts , there has been a 

fradulent withdrawal of an amount of Rs. 4 ,40 
lacs resulting in financial loss to the Bank . 
that on 15- 6 -83 you took with malaſiçle intention , 
vouchers dated 25 - 3 -83 from the caftry und ro 
moved unauthorisedly u cheque No. 965551 for 
Rs. 40 , 000 / - therefrom . You took the said vou 
chers from the daftry on the pleu of tallying Bills 
Payable Balances hut it is noted that therc was no 

differences on said late . 
4 . That the aforesaid acts alleged against you are 

serious acts of gross unisconduct amounting to : 
( a ) Committing acts prejudicial to the injerest of 

the Bank . 
( b ) Dishonesty in connection with Bank s business 

resulting in serious linancial loss to the Bank . 
( c ) Wilful alteration of records with dishonest in 

tention . 
( d ) Grogs ncgligence in the discharge of your 

duties." 
3. The departmental enquiry was instituted to probe into 
the above charges , Shri B . P . Singhal was appointed as En 
quiry Officer, who after holding the enquiry bound charges 
No . 4 ( a ) , 4 ( b ) and 4 ( d ) proved while charge No . 4 ( c ) not 
proved . However, the Disciplinary Authority disagreeing 
with the Enguiry Officer held ibc concerned workman guilty 
of charge No . 4 ( e ) also . 


10 . Hence held that the enquiry was quite fair. and 
proper . 

11 , I have also closely perused the findings of the Enquiry 
Officer and I have come to the conclusion that the same are 
basco on appropriate assessment of the evidence on record . 
The Enquiry Omcer has given adequate reasons for conclu . 
sions arrived at by hini. Hence , held that the findings of 

the Enquiry Officer are not perverse . 
. 12 . Now , let me examine 118 10 whether the punishment 
awarded to the concerned workman was disproportionate 
to the charges found proved against him . It is true that the 
Bank has incurred financial loss to the tune of Rs. 4 .40 
lakhs on account of contributory gross negligence of the 
concerned workinun on duty . It is also hornc out from the 
evidence on record that the concerned workman could not 
SCINonably justify the source of his assets disproportionate 
to his known income may be in the joint name of his wife 

or other family members . It is also true that the past ser 
, vice record of the concerned workman too was not clean as 
he had been awarded punishment for his fradulent act in 
11961 and subsequently in 1973 also he had been awarded 
punishment. 
113 . But on the inaterial on record before me, I um of 
The view that it will meet the ends of justice if the punish 
ment of dismissal from service is reduced to discharge from 
Service under clause 21 ( iv ) ( b ) of the Bipartite Settlement 
dated 1 .4 -2 - 1995 . The Management was fully justified in losing 
confidence in the concerned workman . After all , Bank is 
a financial institution in public sector , wherein , transparent 
honest conduct is the first and the last condition of the 
Service . Looking into the past service record of the concer 
ned workman , no more stake could be taken by retaining him 
in Service any further. 


14 . Hence , held that the action of the management in dis 
missing the concerned workmin frona service vide order 
dated 26 - 11 - 1984 might have bes justified . had the fore 
mentioned Bipartite Settlement, dated 14 2 - 1995 would have 
not come into force , which provides remcdy short of dismis 

sal from service , when it is not in the interest of the institu 
ition to retain an employce like the concerned workman , 


15 . Therefore , the punishment of dismissal from service 
of the workman concerned is reduced to discharge from ser 
vice in terms of Cause 21 ( iv ) ( b ) of 1lie afore -mentionel 
Settlement, dated 14 - 2 - 1995, 29 7 result of which the concer 
ned workman and / or his substituted lexal heirs are entitled 
to superannuation benefits as would have been due otherwise 
at that stage , 1. c . 26 - 11- 198 -4 . 


16 . The case law cited by the concerned workman have 
been gonc through by me, and these have no hearing on the 
facts in the present case before me. 

17. The Management of Puniah National Bank , New Delhi, 
vide their petition dated 9 . 2 - 1987 , proved for their substitu 
tion in place of Hindustan Cominarcial Bank Limited , the sonic 
having been taken over hy the Punjab National Bank , which 
was allowed by the. 


4 . By an order dated 26 - 11 - 1984, the concerned work 
man way dismissed from service of the Bank , which has 
been challenged by the concerned workman on the ground 
that the departmental cnquiry, was not fair and the findings 
of the enquiry are based on suspicion , conjuctures and sur 
mises, which cannot take place of proof. Tho concerned 
workman has also alleged that the punishment awarded to 
him was highly disproportionate . These allegations have 
been denied by the management. 

5 . Out of the pleadings of the concerned parties, follow 
ing issues were framed om 

( 1 ) Whether the Enquiry was fair and proper ? 

( 2 ) As in the terms of reference . 
6 . The management have filed 11 documents along with 
record of the departmental enquiry and have examined 
Shri A , k . Goel, Officer on Special Duty at Chandigarh , 
as MW ) . 

7. The concerned workman has filed 12 documents and 
has examined himself as WWI. 

8 . Heard both the parties and perused the evidence on 
record 

9 . In the departmental enquiry , the concerned workman 
15 allowed to appoint defence representative of his choice , 
fe was also allowed cross - examination of the management 
witnesses He was also supplied copies of relevant docui 
monts. He was also allowed insnection of documents which , 
on course , were availabir with the management and he was 
also allowed to lead evidence in his defencc . I do not find 
nov infirmitive in the enquiry. On the contrary it is clear 
from the proceedings on record that the enquiry has been 
held according to the principles of natural justice with full 

tunity of defence to the concerned workroen , 


18 . The concerned workmai had expired on 17- 9 - 1993 , 
hence his lenn ] heirs, 13Amely Shri Pradeen Mathur , Rekha 
Mallur, Kamla Rani and Suresh Kumar Mathur . moved a 
petition on 4 - 12 - 1993, praying for their substitution in place 
of concerned workmon in the present cose , which was also 
allowed by me. 

19 . Award is given accordingly . 

Further it is ordered that he requisite number of copies 
of this award mov he forwarded to the Central Government 
for necessary nction at their end .

2 . The workm 11 Shri B . K Desai was admittedly working 
as Head Cashier in the C . P . Tank Branch of the Bank of 
Baroda between the period 16th March , 1979 to 16th January , 
1984 . On 10- 9 . 1981, he is said to have handled cash transac 
tions of the Bank at the said Branch and admittedly received 
a sum of Rs. 10 ,00, 000 ten lacs from the Main Office of the 
Bank at Bombay. 


3. It is alleged that on that very day, the workman mis 
Appropriated a suin of Rs. 4 ,00 , 000 four lacs , duly entrusted 
to him and the modus operandi adopted was that he prepared 
a fictitious debit voucher purporting to show a cash remittance 
of Rs. 4 lacs the same day to the Main Office of the Bank , 
cven though no slich rcmittance had in fact been made. The 
workman is said to have destroyed the concerned record with 
a view to cover up this misappropriation . It is further 
alleged that the matter came to light when inspite of re 
peated letters from tho main office , the discrepancy of 
Rs. 4 lacs remittance allegedly having been made, was not 
explained. It is also alleged that normally such a fact could 
have been discovered from cash receipt and payment register 
of the branch office but the workman , to cover up hig 
misdeod made alternations in the relevant entries of the 
said book , so that the discrepency could not be detected 
from the cash balance struck in the said book at the end 
of tho transactions . In contemplation of a regular disci 
plinary enquiry , the workman was placed under suspension 
vide order dated July , 1985 issued by the competent autuhority . 
A chargeshcet dated 6 - 12 - 1985 was duly served on the work 
mon in the following terms ;

11 . There can be no munnor of doubt, nor thoro is any 
doubt in my mind but the charges for wluch the domestic 
enquiry was held did amount to Oriences as venned in clause 
19 . of the Bipartite Selliemcat. Misappropriation /embezzle 
ment of money by a cashier of the Bank ay also destruction 
01 evidence with a view to screen the ouender are both 
offences invoiving moral turpituae of the highest order , 


12 . In the present case , admittedly an FIR of the incident 
was lodged with the police , though the same has not been 
placed on record by either side . On behalf of the union , a 
grievance was made that this FIR or complaint lodged with 
tho police has not been supplied to the workman and this 
hag Vitiated the enquiry . I shall touch on this aspect later, 
but suffice to say that both the sides during oral arguments 
very frankly and candidly conceded that the managemont did 
lodge an FIR with the concerned police station . Thus, the 
Bank admittedly took steps for prosecuting the workman as 
required by clause 19. 3 (a ) of the Bipartite Settlement. 


" The next question is whether the chargc of being in 

pos . ession of assets (usproportionatc to his known 
source of income is a misconduct. Section 5 ( 1) (e ) 
of the Act (which is equivalent to Section 13( 1 ) (0 ) 
of the l revention of Corrupuon Act , 1988 ) deunes 
" criminal misconduct" . A public servant is sind 
to commit ihe olences of criminal misconduct if 
he or any person on his behalt is in possession or 
has, at any time during the period 01 tag ontice , 
been in possession , for which the public sex vant 
cannot satisfactorily account for . Thus, pecuniary 
resources of property disproportionate to his known 
Source of incomnc is criminal misconduct. In 
the 1988 Act an explanation has been added to 
Section 13 ( 1 )( e ) to explain that " known sources of 
income" means income received from any lawful 
source and such receipt has been intimatect in ac 
cordance with tho provision of law , rules or orders 
for the time being applicable to a public servant. 
The charged officer must be a pubiic servant. Ho 
must be found to be in possession of, by himself, 
OT through any person on his behalf, at any timo 
during the period of his ollice , pecuniary resources 
or property disproportionate to his known source 
of income. If he cannot satisfactorily account 
thereof, he is said to have committed criminal mis 
conduct. No doubt it is a presumptive finding but 
that finding is based on three facts. Being a public 
servant, if at any time, during the period of his 
office , he is proved to have been in possession, by 
himself or through any person on his behalf, or 
pecuniary resources or properly disproportionate to 
his known source of income, he is enjoined to 
satisfactorily account for the same. If he fails to 
Account for, he commits misconduct. Therefore , 
as in a prosecution laid under Section 5 ( 1 ) (e ) of 
the Act ( equivalent to Section 13 ( 1 )( c ) of 1988 
Act), a public servant is liable to punishment. The 
need to inake this misconduct expressly a part 
of enumerated items of misconduct under Central 
Civil Services , CA Rules is obviated ." 


13 . This is admitted on both hands that the prosecution 
launched by the Bank was abortive . The workman was 
nitther tried nor convicted not acquitted . Actually , the 
Tribunal is not aware as to what sort of final report of 
investigation was filed by the police . Actually , a grievance 
has been mado by the workman on this score also viz . that 
the report of the police investigation was with held by 
the management. More of this later and sufice to say that 
the workman was not put to actual trial, hence there was 
neither conviction por acquittal for the twin chargey by 
a court of competent jurisdiction . Hence , the provisions 
of clause 19. 3 (b ), (c ) and (d ) are pot attracted to the present 
case . In such an event, provisions of clauso 19 . 4 are squarely 
attracted and a discretion has been Vested in the manage 
ment to treat the offence as gross misconduct or minor 
misconduct as the case may be. This is inspite of the fact 
that guch acts are not specifically enumerated as gross mis 
conduct in clause 19 . 4 . However, it would be seen that 
the two charges would be evidently coverod by sub - clauscs 
( d ) and (j) of clause 19 . 3, Misappropriation / embezzlement of 
funds of the Bank is definitely an act prejudicial to the 
interests of the Bank resulting in serious lose to the bank ; 
Likewise , the various documents / registery pertaining to tran 
actions of cash in the Bank are definitely property of the 
Bankud removel /destruction of such documents is species 
of wlful daraage to the property of the Bank . 


15 . Hence , I am of the view that dictum in Rasik Veghja 
is not applicable to the present case . B . C . Chaturvedi would 
he definitely attracted to this case. Marcover, as I have 
demonstrated earlier, both the charges would fall within 
enumerated gross-misconducts. It would be a highly ludicrous 
promosition to assert that mis appropriation of funds of bank 
and destruction / removal of documents to conceal such act 
of misappropriation , would not amount to gross misconduct. 
Actually the proposition deserved to be stated only for the 
suke of rejection and has no merit inspite of the fact that 
Mr. Vaidya very perserveringly tried to canvass this proposi 
tion with all facility at his wmmand . Moreover, an offence 
could be treated as gro * misconduct or minor misconduct 
by the management by virtue of clause 19 .4 of the Bipartito 
Settlement. Honce, looked from any angle, the contention 
is specious and devoid of merit .

22. Now , the next question is if the findings of 
· the Enquiry Officer are perverse and are not based 

on any acceptable evidence . In this context, it 
is urged that a workman can not be held guilty 
merely on basis of suspecion or on basis of infe 
rences ( 1978 LIC 1106 Nand Kishore Prasad ) 
The principle , so far as it goes is unexceptionable . 
However, it is to be borne in mind that standard 
of proof in domestic enquiries , is different from 
that in criminal cases. However, people can tell 
lies but circumstances do not. It is not disputed 
before me that a sum of 10 , 00 , 000 (ten lacs ) 
were received by the workman from the Head 
Office , in his capacity as Head Cashier. It is in 
the evidence of v . S . Kulkarni that documents 
marked in the enquiry as M2 to M5 were in the 
handwriting of the workman . The witness , in 
answer to questions put to him , explained the 
entries in these documents as follows :- - 
" P . O .- - Can you identify whose writing is it 

in the document M -5 ? 
MW - 2 . - This is the handwriting of Mr. B . K . 

Desai. 
P . O . - - Whose initial is there on the docu 

ment M - 5 ? 
MW - 2 .— This is the initial of Mr. B . K . 

Desai, 
P . O . — Can you tell me what that amount of 

Rs. 1359014 .61 as appears on the exhi 

bit M -5 represent ? 
MW -2 - - This amount represents total cash 

payment made on 10 - 9 - 1981. 
P . O .-- Showing the exhibit M -3 the P . O . 

asked the MW - 2 as to what does BMO 

Alc in general ledger represent ? 
MW -2 , -- This is the daily debit and credit 

transaction routed through BMO A \c 

which are posted in the general ledger . 
P . 0 . - On Exhibit M -2 can you tell me as 

to what the amount of Rs. in cash 

column represents ? 
MW -2 . — This represents amount received by 

the branch from BMO. 
P . O . - - Can you tell me what the amount 

posted in G |L Alcon debit side on 

10 - 9 - 1981 represents ? 
MW - 2 . — This amount represents total debit 

transactions routed through BMO A | c. 
P . O . - - From where this amount was posted ? 
MW - 2 . – From the cash book . 
P . O . - - Was there any difference between the

in the GL on 10 - 9 - 1981. 


MW -2 .- Yes I find there is difference of

MW - 2 — It appears that the difference is in 

cash column as total of transfer and 
clearing columns tally with the credit 

side of the cash book," 
This clearly shows that there was a shortfall of 
ks. 4 lacs in the books. There is an overwriting 
in M -5 , which balances the shortfall and the work 
man did not care to explain this overwriting. The 
overwriting was obviously an attempt to balance 
the entries with a view to cover the embezzeled 
amount of Rs. 4 lacs. The thrust of the cross 
examination of this witness was to show that the 
accountant was a joint custodian of the cash along 
with the head cashier . But, the witness has cate 
gorically explained " As per the practice Accoun 
tant is the joint custodian of the cast i. e . after the 
cash is balanced and kept in cash safe till next 
day morning cash is taken out and handed over 
to the Head Cashier." This makes it abundantly 
clear that the joint custody commences after thc 
cash is balanced and continues till it is in cash safe . 
This gives the clue why the workman made over 
writings in M -5 to balance the cash , which was 
short by exactly 4 lacs. It is in evidence that 
Manager had only a supervisory role to play. I 
have carefully gone through the evidence on re 
cord of domestic enquiry and find that there is 
overwhelming circunstantial evidence to show 
that the workman misappropriated a sum of 4 lacs 
and altered the final untries with a view to avoid 
detection of the said misappropriation . The find 
ing is niether perverse , nor based in inferences 
or surmises, but is based on solid evidence. It was 
contended that the Head Cashier could not have 
embezzled the amount without collection of the 
Accountant or the Manager. I have already re 
ferred to the limited role that the accountant 
plays in the joint custody of cash of course , when 
remittance is made from branch to head office , 
the Accountant and the Manager authenticate the 
voucher but in the present case there is nothing 
to show that sum of Rs. 4 lacs had been at all 
sent to the Head Office . Preparation of a voucher 
by Head Cashier and its authentication by Accour 
tant and Manager would not establish that really 
there was any remittance. Here , I may reproduce 
the reasoning of the Enquiry Oficer has stated

AWARD 
1. The appropriate Government has referred the following 
dispute for adjudication to this Tribunal : 
“ Whether the action of the Management of Bank of 

Baroda, Pune, Sangli in not assigning the duties of 
Agricultural Assistant (with payment of special allow 
ancc ) to Shri 11, T . Vaje , Agricultural Clerk is 
justifiod ? If nut, to what relief is the said work 

man entitled 7" 
2. The facts giving rise to this dispute lie in a narrow 
compas . .

Clerk vido Appointment letter dated April 19 , 1982 under 
the respondent Bank . He was to be on probation for six 
inonths, which period way liable to be extended by the

the post on the expiry of the Probationary period , if his 
work , conduct etc . were found to be satisfactory . Accord

15 . Perused the case record and the evidence 
both oral and documentary advanced on behalt 
of the parties and their points of argument, I fin « l 
much force in the plea taken by the management 
that if the workmen were engaged by the manage 
ment for running the canteen they would have got 
sonie papers to show that actually they were in 
the employment of the management or that the 
Management askod them to run the cancen for 
which articles were supplic by the managenicit 
and sale proceed was given deposited in the account 
of the management. But not a chit of paper has 
been produced on behalf of the workmen and 3t 
the initial stage this dispute was also not spurisorci 
by the union before thic A .L .C . ( C ) which is clear 
from the 13tters addressed by the A .L .C . ( C ) to 
the management and reply given by the manage 
went thereunder. I also find much force in the 
plea of the management that the concerned work 
men have claimed to be Manager and Salern :: . of 
the said canteen , but there is nothing to show to 
corroborate this fact from any picce of document 
So far Ext. W - 7 and Ext. M - 5 is concerned is 
document clearly states that they were running 
the canteen on contact basis meaning thereby 
that they would never be in employment of the 
management as thic canteen was run by thei ? On 
contract basis . MW - 1 has stated specifically 
that he issued certificate Ext. M - 1 at the instance 
of the General Manager of the Project that the 
werkmen required the same for running the can 
toen of the railway. I further find that ceilinly 
their father was working as Munshi in the Project 
and it is just possible that he was running Tea 
Stall in the premises of the Project cutside the 
work site like other lta Stall run privately and 
somehow he could procure certificate from MW - 1 
and the Marager which are Ext. 1 - 7 and lixt 
M -5 and only on the basis of these documents it 
can t be established that these wirkmen ucre 
working in a canteen nun departmentally by the 
management for the period to which they have 
claimed . Certainly there was never any rela 
tionship of emplover and emplovees between the 
parties at any point of time which also gets sup 
port from Ext. W - 7 and Ext. M -5 . In this view 
of the matter I find that the action of the manage 
ment in not providing employment to the work 
men or remilarising their service w . e f. 1 - 1 -81 is 
fullu iustified and the concerned workmen are not 
entitled for the relief as claimed . Both points are 
decided accordingly .

19 . I find much force in the plea taken by the 
workman and from the entries made in all the rel: 
vant document his date of birth was 8 - 6 - 1936 and all

to the workman Naturally , if there was any doubt 
in the records of the nianagemcnt then benefit of such

those clocuments and the reply of the C . M . P . F . Com 
missioner that such register and documents were not 
available in the oflice also , then what reliance can be 
placed cu the contention of the management and that 
really hc was 33 years of age on 1971 and by calcula 
tion he would be completing 60 years of age on 
1 - 7- 1993 on which date he has been superannuated . 


20 . In the circumstances , as discussed above , I find 
that the action of the management in superannuating 
the concerned workmar Shri Barmeshwar Lall , Ex 
Leave Clerk w . e.f. 1- 7 - 1993 is not at all justified and 
it cannot be said acted in good faith and this action 
of the management is arbitrary and malafide . Accord 
ingly , the workman is entitled to the relief that he be 
superannuated on completion of 60 years of age on 
8 -6 - 1996 . It is to be noted that ever on that date 
his date has expired and he had been superannuated 
on rcaching 8 -6 - 1996 as per his date of birth taking. 
to be correct as 8 - 6 - 1936 and so be would be entitled 
for monctany benefits of warcs and other benefits 
for the period from 1 - 7 - 1993 to 8 - 6 - 1966 . 
after calculating his full back wages, in 
crement and other benefits had he been in service till 
8 - 6 - 1996 . Hence , the following Award is render

7 . I am not inclined to helicve the version of the 
management as it has failed to produce the relevant 
attendance register to show the number of working 
days of the concerned workma . As observed earlici 
Ext. M - 1 and M - 2 the attendance register do not 
relate to the period in question lience they are not of 
any help . Thus drawing adverse inference against 
the management because of failure to fill attendance 
register. I accept the evidence of workman and hold 
that he had worked for more 240 days in a year . 
Admittedly no notice pay and retrenchment compen 
sation was given to him , hence his termination is had 
in breach of Section 25- F of I. D . Act .

President 

Doordarshan. Karamchari Congress, 
1 Abdul Aziz Road , Lucknow . 


8 . There is no prove for substantiating the breach 
of provision of Section 25 - H of I. D . Act . 


AWARD 


9 . In the end my award is that the termination of 
the concerned workman is bad in law and he is entitled 
for reinstatement with back wages as a daily rated 
worker at the rate at which he was drawing wages 
for the last time.

वेतन सहित छुट्टी अथवा 40 दिन की अर्ध-वेमम सहित 
छुट्टी प्रदान करेगी । 

22. मी० एट एफ ० व्यवस्थाओं का समाप्त किया जाना 
14. छुट्टियों का सचयन : 
कंपनी, कर्मचारियों को अधिकतम 30 दिन अजित छुट्टी तथा 

कंपनी, कंपनी में विद्यमान सी० एण्ड एफ० एजेन्टों को वापस लेगी 
सेवा की संपूर्ण प्रधि की ग्णता छुटटी उपयुक्त करने की अनमति देगी । 

और सो० एण्ड एफ० एजेन्टों के माध्यम से कंपनी के लिए कार्य कर रहे 
15. छुट्टि यों का नकदीकरण : 

इन सभी कर्मचारियों को :- कंपनी के स्थायी कर्मचारियों की तरह सभी 

परिणामी लाभों और विशेषाधिकारों के साथ कंपनी के नियमित कर्म 
कम्पनी , कर्मचारियों को न ली गयी अजित छुट्टी का वर्ष में एक 

चारियों के रूप में समाहित करेगी । 
बार भुगतान प्राप्त करने तथा आणता छुट्टी का सेवा प्रबंधि समाप्त होने 
पर भुगतान प्राप्त करने की अनुमति देगी । 

23. संघ के सदस्यों का स्थानांतरण : 
16: उपदान 

जम तक परिसंघ सहमत नहीं हो जाता कंपनी द्वारा इस परिसंघ 
____ कम्पनी, सेवानिवृत होने वाले त्यागपत्र देने वाले अथवा कम्पनी के 

अथवा इसकी संघटक इकाई के किसी सवस्य को स्थानांतरित नहीं किय 
रोजगार में न रहने वाले कर्मचारियों की सेवा के परे किए गए प्रत्येक 

जाएगा । 
वर्ष के लिए 60 विन पी दर से उपधान के कप में प्रमिनल वेतन का 24 अवमाम मघ छेन सुविधाएं : 
भुगतान करेगी ।
